where:

\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p},\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}}:\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\{{a+\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}\rightarrow k_{\mathfrak{p}}(A.)

By the universal property of
\eta_{\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}
, it follows that

, hence

and

is unique.

We have now the following commutative diagram:

\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

\textstyle{\mathop{\rm colim}_{a\notin\mathfrak{p}}\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\left\{{a+\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\right\}}

Remark 4

Since
\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\subseteq\mathfrak{p}
, it follows that
q_{b,\mathfrak{p}}
is surjective and since
\bigcup{\rm im}\,\jmath_{a}=\mathop{\rm colim}_{a\notin\mathfrak{p}}\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\{{a+\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}
(because it is a colimit and the right triangle of the above diagram commutes), it will follow that
\bigcup{\rm im}\,f^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}=\mathop{\rm colim}_{a\notin\mathfrak{p}}\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\{{a+\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}
. Hence

\left\{f^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}:\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\rightarrow\mathop{\rm colim}_{a\notin\mathfrak{p}}\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\{{a+\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}|\mathfrak{p}\in D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\right\}

is collectively surjective;

Let
a,a^{\prime},b,b^{\prime}\in A
be such that
D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\subseteq D^{\infty}(a^{\prime})\cap Z^{\infty}(b^{\prime})
. Then

D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\subseteq Z^{\infty}(b^{\prime})

(4)

and

D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\subseteq D^{\infty}(a^{\prime})

(5)

We claim that (
4
) implies that
\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}\subseteq\ker(t_{a,b})
.

It suffices to show that:

\bigcap D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\subseteq\ker(t_{a,b}),

for

\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}=\bigcap Z^{\infty}(b^{\prime})\subseteq\bigcap D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b).

We have:

\bigcap D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)=\{x\in A|(\forall\mathfrak{p}\in{\rm Spec}^{\infty}\,(A))(a\notin\mathfrak{p})\wedge(b\in\mathfrak{p})\to(x\in\mathfrak{p})\}

t_{a,b}(x)=\eta^{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}_{\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}(q_{b}(x))=0\iff(\exists\theta\in\{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}})(\theta\cdot q_{b}(x)=0\,\,{\rm in}\,\,\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}})

Claim:
x\in\bigcap D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\rightarrow a\cdot x\in\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}
.

Ab absurdo
, suppose
b\in\mathfrak{p}
and
a\cdot x\notin\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}
. Since
\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}=\bigcap Z^{\infty}(b)
, there must exist some
\mathfrak{p}\in Z^{\infty}(b)
such that
a\cdot x\in\mathfrak{p}
. Since
\mathfrak{p}
is a prime ideal, it follows that
a\notin\mathfrak{p}
and
x\notin\mathfrak{p}
.

Thus we have a contradiction between
(x\in\bigcap D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b))
and
(\exists\mathfrak{p}\in{\rm Spec}^{\infty}\,(A))((a\notin\mathfrak{p})\wedge(b\in\mathfrak{p})\wedge(x\notin\mathfrak{p}))
.

Now,

t_{a,b}(x)=\eta_{\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}(q_{b}(x))\cdot\underbrace{\eta_{\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}(q_{b}(a))}_{\in\left(\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\right)^{\times}}=\eta^{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}_{\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}(q_{b}(a\cdot x))=\eta_{\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}(0)=0

so
x\in\bigcap D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)
implies
x\in\ker(t_{a,b})
,
i.e.
,

\bigcap D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\subseteq\ker(t_{a,b}),

hence

\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}\subseteq\bigcap D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\subseteq\ker(t_{a,b})

\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}\subseteq\ker(t_{a,b}).

By the
Theorem of the
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
Homomorphism
, there is a unique
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
homomorphism
r^{b^{\prime}}_{a,b}:\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\to\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}
such that
r^{b^{\prime}}_{a,b}\circ q_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}=t_{a,b}
, that is, such that the following diagram commutes:

\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{q_{b^{\prime}}}
\scriptstyle{t_{a,b}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{r^{b^{\prime}}_{a,b}}

Now we claim that there is a unique
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
homomorphism
t^{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}_{a,b}:\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}\to\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}
such that the following diagram commutes:

\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}}
\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{q_{b}}
\scriptstyle{q_{b^{\prime}}}
\scriptstyle{t_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}}
\scriptstyle{t_{a,b}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}}
\scriptstyle{r^{b^{\prime}}_{a,b}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\exists!t^{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}_{a,b}}

We claim that (
5
) implies that
r^{b^{\prime}}_{a,b}(a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})})\in\left(\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\}\right)^{\times}
. This is equivalent to assert that
t_{a,b}(a^{\prime})\in\left(\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\}\right)^{\times}
or that
q_{b}(a^{\prime})\in\{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}}
.

Ab absurdo
, suppose that
q_{b}(a^{\prime})\notin\{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}}\subseteq\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}
. By
Theorem 21
of page 89 of
[
6
]
, there is some
\mathfrak{P}\in{\rm Spec}^{\infty}\,\left(\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)
(
\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\subseteq\mathfrak{P}
) such that
q_{b}(a^{\prime})\in\mathfrak{P}
and
a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\notin\mathfrak{P}
. Since
{\rm Spec}^{\infty}\,\left(\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\cong{\rm Spec}^{\infty}_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\,(A)=\{\mathfrak{s}\in{\rm Spec}^{\infty}\,(A)|\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\subseteq\mathfrak{s}\}
, there is a unique
\mathfrak{p}\in{\rm Spec}^{\infty}_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\,(A)
such that
\mathfrak{P}=\mathfrak{p}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}
and
\mathfrak{p}\in Z^{\infty}(b)
(because
b\in\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\subseteq\mathfrak{P}=\mathfrak{p}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}
).

Since
q_{b}(a^{\prime})\in\mathfrak{P}=\mathfrak{p}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}
and
a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\notin\mathfrak{P}=\mathfrak{p}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}
, it follows that
a^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}
(
\mathfrak{p}\in Z^{\infty}(a^{\prime})
) and
a\notin\mathfrak{p}
(
\mathfrak{p}\in D^{\infty}(a)
). Since
b\in\mathfrak{p}
(
\mathfrak{p}\in Z^{\infty}(b)
), it follows that:

\mathfrak{p}\in D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\cap Z^{\infty}(a^{\prime}),

hence

D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\cap Z^{\infty}(a^{\prime})\neq\varnothing

D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\nsubseteq D^{\infty}(a^{\prime}),

which contradicts (
5
).

Hence:

q_{b}(a^{\prime})\in\{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}}

and by the universal property of
\eta_{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}:\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\rightarrow\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}
, there exists a unique
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
homomorphism
t^{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}_{a,b}:\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}\rightarrow\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}
such that the following triangle commutes:

\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{r^{b^{\prime}}_{a,b}}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t^{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}_{a,b}}

Now we claim that the following diagram is a colimit:

\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\left\{\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}^{-1}\right\}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\right)\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{f^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\scriptstyle{t^{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}_{a,b}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{f^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}}

In order to show that, three conditions must hold:

1)

For every
a,b,a^{\prime},b^{\prime}
such that
\mathfrak{p}\in D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)
and
\mathfrak{p}\in D^{\infty}(a^{\prime})\cap Z^{\infty}(b^{\prime})
the above diagram commutes;

2)

The family
\{f^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}:\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\rightarrow\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\left\{{\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}}^{-1}\right\}\}
is collectively surjective;

3)

Given
a_{1},b_{1},a_{2},b_{2}\in A
such that
D^{\infty}(a_{2})\cap Z^{\infty}(b_{2})\subseteq D^{\infty}(a_{1})\cap Z^{\infty}(b_{1})
,
\theta_{1}\in\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b_{1})}}\right)\{{a_{1}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b_{1})}}^{-1}\}
and
\theta_{2}\in\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b_{2})}}\right)\{{a_{2}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b_{2})}}^{-1}\}
such that
t^{a_{1},b_{1}}_{\mathfrak{p}}(\theta_{1})=t^{a_{2},b_{2}}_{\mathfrak{p}}(\theta_{2})
, there are
a,b\in A
such that
\mathfrak{p}\in D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\subseteq D^{\infty}(a_{2})\cap Z^{\infty}(b_{2})
and
t^{a_{1},b_{1}}_{a,b}(\theta_{1})=t^{a_{2},b_{2}}_{a,b}(\theta_{2})
, where
t^{a_{1},b_{1}}_{a,b}
and
t^{a_{2},b_{2}}_{a,b}
are given in the following commutative diagram:

\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b_{1})}}\right)\{{a_{1}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b_{1})}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t^{a_{1},b_{1}}_{a_{2},b_{2}}}
\scriptstyle{t^{a_{1},b_{1}}_{a,b}}
\scriptstyle{t^{a_{1},b_{1}}_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\left\{{\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}}^{-1}\right\}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b_{2})}}\right)\{{a_{2}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b_{2})}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t^{a_{2},b_{2}}_{a,b}}
\scriptstyle{t^{a_{2},b_{2}}_{\mathfrak{p}}}

By
Remark
4
, the condition 2) is already satisfied.

Note that for each basic open subset
U
of
{\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A)
,
F_{A}(U)
has the structure of an
A-
algebra. The universal property of

F_{A}(U)=\varprojlim_{\begin{subarray}{c}U=D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)\\
(a,b)\in A\times A\end{subarray}}\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}

induces a unique
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
homomorphism
u:A\to F_{A}(U)
such that the following diagram commutes:

\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t_{a,b}}
\scriptstyle{t_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}}
\scriptstyle{\exists!u}
\textstyle{F_{A}(U)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t^{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}_{a,b}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}}

for every
a,a^{\prime},b,b^{\prime}\in A
such that
D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)=U=D^{\infty}(a^{\prime})\cap Z^{\infty}(b^{\prime})
.

Now let
U
and
V
be any two basic open sets of
{\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A)
such that
U\subseteq V
, and let
\iota^{V}_{U}:U\hookrightarrow V
be the inclusion map. Since
U
and
V
are basic open subsets of the constructible topology, there are
a,b,c,d\in A
such that
U=D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)
and
V=D^{\infty}(c)\cap Z^{\infty}(d)
. Let
a^{\prime},b^{\prime},c^{\prime},d^{\prime}\in A
be such that
U=D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)=D^{\infty}(a^{\prime})\cap Z^{\infty}(b^{\prime})
and
V=D^{\infty}(c)\cap Z^{\infty}(d)=D^{\infty}(c^{\prime})\cap Z^{\infty}(d^{\prime})
, so:

t^{a,b}_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}:\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\rightarrow\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}

and

t^{c,d}_{c^{\prime},d^{\prime}}:\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}\{{c+\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}^{-1}\}\rightarrow\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(d^{\prime})}}\{{c^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(d^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}

are
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
isomorphisms. Consider the following commutative diagram:

where

is the unique

,

We have, then, the following cone:

\textstyle{F_{A}(U)}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}\{{c+\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

\scriptstyle{t^{c,d}_{c^{\prime},d^{\prime}}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(d^{\prime})}}\{{c^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(d^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\jmath_{a,b}\circ f^{c^{\prime},d^{\prime}}_{a,b}}

that induces, by the universal property of
F_{A}(V)
as a colimit, a
unique

\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
homomorphism
f^{U}_{V}:F_{A}(V)\to F_{A}(U)
such that for every
a,b,c,d,c^{\prime},d^{\prime}\in A
the following diagram commutes:

\textstyle{F_{A}(V)}
\textstyle{F_{A}(U)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{f^{U}_{V}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}\{{c+\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\jmath_{c,d}\circ f^{c,d}_{a,b}}
\scriptstyle{k_{c,d}}
\scriptstyle{t^{c,d}_{c^{\prime},d^{\prime}}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(d^{\prime})}}\{{c^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(d^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{k_{c^{\prime},d^{\prime}}}
\scriptstyle{\jmath_{a,b}\circ t^{c^{\prime},d^{\prime}}_{a,b}}

where

and

are the canonical colimit
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
homomorphisms.

Let
f^{U}_{c,d}:=\jmath^{U}_{a,b}\circ f^{c,d}_{a,b}
,
f^{U}_{c,d}:=\jmath^{U}_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}\circ f^{c^{\prime},d^{\prime}}_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}
,

and
f^{V}_{e^{\prime},f^{\prime}}:=\jmath^{V}_{c^{\prime},d^{\prime}}\circ\jmath^{e^{\prime},f^{\prime}}_{c^{\prime},d^{\prime}}
.

\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\jmath^{U}_{a,b}}
\textstyle{F_{A}(U)}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\jmath^{U}_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}}
\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t_{a,b}}
\scriptstyle{t_{c,d}}
\scriptstyle{t_{e,f}}

Denote

\scriptstyle{\jmath^{U}_{a,b}}

commutes

By the universal property of
F_{A}(W)
, since

there is a unique
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-

\scriptstyle{\jmath^{W}_{e^{\prime},f^{\prime}}}

Hence, by the uniqueness of

, it follows that
f^{U}_{W}=f^{U}_{V}\circ f^{V}_{W}
.

Given the inclusion
\iota^{U}_{U}={\rm id}_{U}:U\hookrightarrow U
, by the universal property of the colimit, there is a unique
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
homomorphism

such that

:

\textstyle{F_{A}(U)}
\textstyle{F_{A}(U)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

In particular, if we take

and

, we will have

, so there is a unique
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
homomorphism

such that the following diagram commutes:

\textstyle{F_{A}(U)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{f^{U}_{U}}
\textstyle{F_{A}(U)}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

Thus, we have a functor from the category of the basic open subsets of
{\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A)
,
(\mathcal{B}({\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}),\subseteq)
to the category of
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
rings:

F_{A}:(\mathcal{B}({\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A)),\subseteq)^{\rm op}\rightarrow\mathcal{C}^{\infty}{\rm Rng}

Given any open set

of
{\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A)
and any
\mathfrak{p}\in U
, there exists some basic open subset
V=D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)
, for some

, such that
\mathfrak{p}\in V\subseteq U
, so
\mathcal{B}({\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A))^{\rm op}
is a cofinal subset of
{\rm Open}\,({\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A))^{\rm op}
.

In order to describe the stalk of this pre-sheaf at
\mathfrak{p}
, it suffices to calculate the colimit over the system
\mathcal{B}({\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A))
:

(F_{A})_{\mathfrak{p}}=\displaystyle\varinjlim_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\in{\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A)\\
\mathfrak{p}\in U\end{subarray}}F_{A}(U)=\displaystyle\varinjlim_{\begin{subarray}{c}V\in\mathcal{B}({\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A))\\
\mathfrak{p}\in V\end{subarray}}F_{A}(V)

We have the following cone:

\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\{\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}^{-1}\}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}\right)\{{d+\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{f^{c,d}_{a,b}}
\scriptstyle{t^{c,d}_{\mathfrak{p}}}

(6)

We claim that:

\begin{subarray}{c}\displaystyle\varprojlim\\
V\in\mathcal{B}({\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A))\end{subarray}F_{A}(V)\cong\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\{{\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}}^{-1}\}

In order to prove it, we must have:

(i)

The diagram (
6
) is a commutative cone;

(ii)

The family of arrows
\{t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}\}_{a,b\in U}
is collectively surjective;

(iii)

For every
x_{a,b}\in\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}
and
x_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}\in\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\right)\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}
such that:

t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}(x_{a,b})=t^{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}_{\mathfrak{p}}(x_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}})

there are
c,d\in A
such that:

D^{\infty}(c)\cap Z^{\infty}(d)\subseteq[D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)]\cap[D^{\infty}(a^{\prime})\cap Z^{\infty}(b^{\prime})],

i.e., there are arrows:

f^{a,b}_{c,d}:\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\to\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}\right)\{{c+\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}^{-1}\}

and

f^{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}_{c,d}:\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\right)\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}\to\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}\right)\{{c+\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}^{-1}\}

such that:

f^{a,b}_{c,d}(x_{a,b})=f^{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}_{c,d}(x_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}})

that is, the following diagram commutes:

\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{f^{a,b}_{c,d}}
\scriptstyle{t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}\right)\{{c+\sqrt[\infty]{(d)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t^{c,d}_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\{\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}^{-1}\}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}\right)\{{a^{\prime}+\sqrt[\infty]{(b^{\prime})}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{f^{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}_{c,d}}
\scriptstyle{t^{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}_{\mathfrak{p}}}

The latter condition is equivalent to saying that for every
a,b\in A
, the morphism:

\begin{array}[]{cccc}t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}:&\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}^{-1}\}&\rightarrow&\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\{\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}^{-1}\}\\
&\gamma&\mapsto&t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}(\gamma)\end{array}

is injective, that is,
\ker t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}=\{0\}
.

Since
t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}=\jmath_{a}\circ\eta_{b,\mathfrak{p}}
and
\jmath_{a}
is injective,
\ker t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}=\ker\eta_{b,\mathfrak{p}}
and showing that
t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}
is injective is equivalent to prove that
\ker\eta_{b,\mathfrak{p}}\{0\}
.

Consider the following diagram:

\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}
\scriptstyle{q_{b,\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{b,\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{q_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}
\scriptstyle{q_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\{\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}^{-1}\}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\{{a+\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\jmath_{a}}

We have:

t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}=\jmath_{a}\circ\eta_{b,\mathfrak{p}}

so

t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}\circ\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}=\jmath_{a}\circ\eta_{b,\mathfrak{p}}\circ\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}

Since
\eta_{b,\mathfrak{p}}\circ\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}=\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}\circ q_{b,\mathfrak{p}}
, it follows that:

t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}\circ\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}=\jmath_{a}\circ\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}\circ q_{b,\mathfrak{p}}

t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}\circ\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\circ q_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}=\jmath_{a}\circ\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}\circ\underbrace{q_{b,\mathfrak{p}}\circ q_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}_{=q_{\mathfrak{p}}}

hence

t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}\circ\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\circ q_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}=\jmath_{a}\circ\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}\circ q_{\mathfrak{p}}

(7)

Let
\gamma\in\ker t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}\subseteq\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}^{-1}\}
, so there are
\alpha+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)},\in\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)
and
\beta+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\in\{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}}
such that
\gamma=\dfrac{\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}(\alpha+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})}{\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}(\beta+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})}
.

Now,
t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}(\gamma)=0
if, and only if,
(t^{a,b}_{\mathfrak{p}}\circ\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\circ q_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}})(\alpha\cdot\beta^{-1})=0
. By (
7
), this last condition is equivalent to:

\jmath_{a}(\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}(q_{\mathfrak{p}}(\alpha\cdot\beta^{-1})))=\jmath(\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}\circ q_{\mathfrak{p}}(\alpha)\cdot\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}\circ q_{\mathfrak{p}}(\beta)^{-1})

so

(\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}\circ q_{\mathfrak{p}})(\alpha)\in\ker\jmath_{a}=\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}

and

\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}(\alpha+\mathfrak{p})=0+\mathfrak{p}.

However,
\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}(\alpha+\mathfrak{p})=0\iff(\exists\delta+\mathfrak{p}\in\{a+\mathfrak{p}\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}})((\delta+\mathfrak{p})\cdot(\alpha+\mathfrak{p})=0+\mathfrak{p})
, equivalently,
\alpha\cdot\delta\in\mathfrak{p}
. Since
\mathfrak{p}
is a prime ideal,
\alpha\cdot\delta\in\mathfrak{p}\to(\alpha\in\mathfrak{p})\vee(\delta\in\mathfrak{p})
.

We claim that
\alpha\in\mathfrak{p}
.
Ab absurdo
, suppose
\alpha\notin\mathfrak{p}
, so we must have
\delta\in\mathfrak{p}
. However,
\delta+\mathfrak{p}\in\{a+\mathfrak{p}\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}}
, so
\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}(\delta+\mathfrak{p})\in\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\{{a+\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}^{\times}
. But since
\delta\in\mathfrak{p}
, it would follow that
\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}(\delta+\mathfrak{p})=\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}(0+\mathfrak{p})\in\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\{{a+\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}^{\times}
, from which would follow that
\left(\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\{{a+\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}\cong\{0\}
, a contradiction.

Thus we have
\alpha\in\mathfrak{p}
, so
\alpha+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}
, so
\alpha+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\in q_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}[\mathfrak{p}]
and
q_{b,\mathfrak{p}}(\alpha+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})=0+\mathfrak{p}
. Hence:

\eta_{b,\mathfrak{p}}(\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}(\alpha+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}))=\eta_{a+\mathfrak{p}}(\alpha+\mathfrak{p})=0

and the result follows.

Now we are going to compute the fibers of the above presheaf.

Theorem 2.5

Consider the presheaf defined on the basis of the constructible topology:

\begin{array}[]{cccc}F_{A}:&{\mathcal{B}}\,({\rm Spec}^{\infty-{\rm const}}\,(A))^{{\rm op}}&\rightarrow&\mathcal{C}^{\infty}{\rm Rng}\\
&U=D^{\infty}(a)\cap Z^{\infty}(b)&\mapsto&F_{A}(U)=\varinjlim_{U=D^{\infty}(a)}\left(\frac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{(a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})^{-1}\}\\
&\imath^{V}_{U}:U\hookrightarrow V&\mapsto&\rho^{V}_{U}:F_{A}(U)\to F_{A}(V)\end{array}

The stalk of
F_{A}
at a point
\mathfrak{p}\in{\rm Spec}^{\infty}\,(A)
is isomorphic to the
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
field
\left(\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\left\{\left(\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}^{-1}\right)\right\}
.

Proof

Let
L=\varinjlim_{\mathfrak{p}\in U_{a,b}}\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{(a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})^{-1}\}
.

Consider the following commutative diagram:

\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{A\{{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}}
\textstyle{L}

where
t=\jmath_{a,b}\circ\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\circ q_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}
.

It is clear that
(x\in A\setminus\mathfrak{p})\to(t(x)\in L^{\times})
. Now, since
\mathfrak{p}\in{\rm Spec}^{\infty}\,(A)
, we have
{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}={A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}^{\infty-{\rm sat}}
, so there is a unique
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
homomorphism
t_{\mathfrak{p}}:A\{{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}\to L
such that the following triangle commutes:

\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{A\{{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{L}

In what follows we are going to show that
t_{\mathfrak{p}}
is a
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
isomorphism.

Consider the diagram:

\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t_{a,b}}
\scriptstyle{q_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}
\scriptstyle{t}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{A\{{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}
\textstyle{L}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{(a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\jmath_{a,b}}

Now we claim that:

t_{\mathfrak{p}}:A\{{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}^{-1}\}\to L
is a surjective map,
i.e.
,

(\forall\gamma\in L)(\exists x\in A)(\exists y\in A\setminus\mathfrak{p})\left(\gamma=\frac{t(x)}{t(y)}=t_{\mathfrak{p}}\left(\frac{\eta_{\mathfrak{p}}(x)}{\eta_{\mathfrak{p}}(y)}\right)\right).

Now, given
\gamma\in L
, since
\jmath_{a,b}
is surjective there must exist
\alpha_{a,b}\in\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{(a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})^{-1}\}
such that
\jmath_{a,b}(\alpha_{a,b})=\gamma
.

Since
\alpha_{a,b}\in\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{(a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})^{-1}\}
, there are
c+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\in\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}
and
d+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\in\{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}}
such that
\alpha_{a,b}=\dfrac{\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}(c+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})}{\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}(d+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})}
.

Now,
d+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\in\{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}}\iff(\exists z\in A)(\exists s\in\{a\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}})(s\cdot(d\cdot z-1)\in\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})
, and since
\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\subseteq\mathfrak{p}
, we have:

(\exists z\in A)(\exists s\in\{a\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}})(s\cdot(d\cdot z-1)\in\mathfrak{p}).

Since
s\in\{a\}^{\infty-{\rm sat}}\subseteq{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}^{\infty-{\rm sat}}=A\setminus\mathfrak{p}
, it follows that
s\notin\mathfrak{p}
, and since
s\cdot(d\cdot z-1)\in\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}\subseteq\mathfrak{p}
and
\mathfrak{p}
is a prime ideal, either
s\in\mathfrak{p}
or
d\cdot z-1\in\mathfrak{p}
. It is not the case that
s\in\mathfrak{p}
so
d\cdot z-1\in\mathfrak{p}
. However, if
d\in\mathfrak{p}
we would have
d\cdot z-1\in\mathfrak{p}
, hence
1\in\mathfrak{p}
and
\mathfrak{p}
would not be a proper ideal, hence
d\in A\setminus\mathfrak{p}={A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}^{\infty-{\rm sat}}
.

It suffices to take:

\alpha_{a,b}=\frac{t_{a,b}(c)}{t_{a,b}(d)},

so

\gamma=\jmath_{a,b}(\alpha_{a,b})=\jmath_{a,b}\left(\frac{t_{a,b}(c)}{t_{a,b}(d)}\right)=\frac{t(c)}{t(d)}=t_{\mathfrak{p}}\left(\frac{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}(c)}{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}(d)}\right)

and
t_{\mathfrak{p}}
is surjective.

Claim:

\mathfrak{m}_{\mathfrak{p}}\subseteq\ker t_{\mathfrak{p}}
.

Let
\frac{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}(x)}{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}(y)}\in\mathfrak{m}_{\mathfrak{p}}
, with
x\in\mathfrak{p}
and
y\in A\setminus\mathfrak{p}
(cf.
Proposition 20
, p. 69 of
[
6
]
). We have that
t_{\mathfrak{p}}\left(\frac{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}(x)}{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}(y)}\right)
.

Now we claim that
t(x)=0
, from what follows that:

t_{\mathfrak{p}}\left(\frac{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}(x)}{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}(y)}\right)=\frac{t(x)}{t(y)}=0.

Since the following diagram commutes for every
a\in A\setminus\mathfrak{p}
and
b\in\mathfrak{p}
:

\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t}
\scriptstyle{t_{a,b}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{(a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\jmath_{a,b}}
\textstyle{L}

in particular, making
a=1\in A\setminus\mathfrak{p}
and
b=x\in\mathfrak{p}
yields the following commutative diagram:

\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{t}
\scriptstyle{t_{1,x}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(x)}}\{(1+\sqrt[\infty]{(x)})^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\jmath_{1,x}}
\textstyle{L}

But
t(x)=\jmath_{1,x}(t_{1,x}(x))=\jmath_{1,x}(\eta_{1+\sqrt[\infty]{(x)}}(0+\sqrt[\infty]{(x)}))=0
, hence
\frac{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}(x)}{\eta_{A\setminus\mathfrak{p}}(y)}\in\ker t_{\mathfrak{p}}
.

By the
Theorem of the Homomorphism
, there exists a unique
\overline{t_{\mathfrak{p}}}:\dfrac{A_{\mathfrak{p}}}{\mathfrak{m}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\to L
such that:

\textstyle{A_{\mathfrak{p}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{q_{\mathfrak{m}_{\mathfrak{p}}}}
\scriptstyle{t_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A_{\mathfrak{p}}}{\mathfrak{m}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\overline{t_{\mathfrak{p}}}}
\textstyle{L}

and since
t_{\mathfrak{p}}=\overline{t_{\mathfrak{p}}}\circ q_{\mathfrak{m}_{\mathfrak{p}}}
, it follows that
\overline{t_{\mathfrak{p}}}
is surjective, hence an epimorphism.

In order to show that
\overline{t_{\mathfrak{p}}}
is a
\mathcal{C}^{\infty}-
isomorphism, since it is already an epimorphism, it suffices to prove that it admits a section.

For every
a,b\in A
such that

and

, consider the following diagram:

\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

\scriptstyle{\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{(a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})^{-1}\}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}}}
\textstyle{\left(\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\left\{\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}^{-1}\right\}}

We have the following commutative diagram:

\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{q_{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}
\scriptstyle{q_{\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

\scriptstyle{\eta_{a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\{(a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{b,\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{\frac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}+\mathfrak{p}}}
\textstyle{\left(\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\left\{\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}+\mathfrak{p}^{-1}\right\}}

Let
a,b,c,d\in A
be such that
U_{c,d}\subseteq U_{a,b}
, so we have the following diagram:

\textstyle{\left(\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\left\{\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}^{-1}\right\}}
\textstyle{\left(\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{(a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

\scriptstyle{t^{c,d}_{\mathfrak{p}}}

By the universal property of the inductive limit
L
, there must exist a unique

\Psi_{\mathfrak{p}}:L\to\left(\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\left\{\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}^{-1}\right\}

such that the following diagram commutes:

\textstyle{\left(\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\right)\left\{\dfrac{A}{\mathfrak{p}}\setminus\{0+\mathfrak{p}\}^{-1}\right\}}
\textstyle{L\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

\textstyle{\left(\dfrac{A}{\sqrt[\infty]{(b)}}\right)\{(a+\sqrt[\infty]{(b)})^{-1}\}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

\scriptstyle{\jmath_{c,d}}

Finally we have the following diagram:

\textstyle{A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

\textstyle{A_{\mathfrak{p}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}

\scriptstyle{\jmath_{a,b}}
